# 全部已确定的后续教学插图任务

共114条独立中英文制作Brief；其中24条针对HTML/CSS知识关系图，其余为图像制作。另35条数学章级计划因无真实小节/归属而冻结，不假造编号或函数参数。203条旧记录加化学6的6条为209条总记录；60条保留、35条冻结、114条需制作/修正。

本文件不调用AI生图。精确科学图默认SVG或数学绘图库；AI结果仅可作草稿。上传6图的审核与11幅确定性总结SVG另见总清单，不能把它们与新Prompt数混算。

来源身份：学校文件仅用于兴华校内；2026董总文件未自动锁定2027；科学辅助来源不提供考频或官方评分。每条Prompt可独立复制，无“同上”。

**Complete English prompt**

Create an original Chinese-language teaching diagram titled "3.1 机械运动：模块03知识结构". Unique ID ILL-MAP-PHY-3_1; Physics, existing chapter 3, actual subsection 3.1 ("机械运动"). Scientific objects, relationships and exact constraints: Branch from research target and scale to whether dimensions may be ignored; preserve both valid and invalid point-mass outcomes. Use labeled relation types rather than one unlabeled downward chain. Required on-canvas Chinese labels, preserved verbatim: 研究对象; 比较尺寸与问题尺度; 质点模型; 位置随时间变化. Use a 960 by 640 canvas. Reflow panels vertically on mobile, with rendered labels at least12 CSS px, target14. Use shape/line style as well as color, white background and high contrast. Keep geometry, arrows and editable text on separate layers. The final scientific output must be constructed with SVG or deterministic mathematical plotting; an AI image is only a layout draft and cannot certify geometry, counts, membranes or formulas. These sources do not certify2027 exam scope or official marking rules.

**Negative Prompt**

不得把分类画成因果、把旁路画成货物必经站、把膜画成不连续碎片，或把示意画成真实比例。不使用乱码、伪官方标志、水印遮挡、未说明的缩写或“同上”。

No classification-as-causation, energy supply as cargo route, disconnected membranes, impossible compartment crossings, or schematic geometry presented as measured scale. No garbled Chinese, fake official logos, obscuring watermarks, unexplained abbreviations or references to another prompt.

Use a 1200 by 800 canvas. Reflow panels vertically on mobile, with rendered labels at least12 CSS px, target14. Use shape/line style as well as color, white background and high contrast. Keep geometry, arrows and editable text on separate layers. Make annotated teaching and blank assessment variants separately, using only existing stated problem data. Scientific objects, relationships and exact constraints: Distinguish active-site competition from the ideal pure noncompetitive model. Noncompetitive is not a synonym for every allosteric, mixed or irreversible inhibition. Separate substrate binding from catalytic activity: an inhibited enzyme need not lose all substrate binding. Keep geometry, arrows and editable text on separate layers. The final scientific output must be constructed with SVG or deterministic mathematical plotting; an AI image is only a layout draft and cannot certify geometry, counts, membranes or formulas. **完整中文Prompt**

为UEC中文原创学习笔记绘制“图 3-7c　O₂ 浓度对呼吸作用的影响（示意）”。唯一ID：ILL-ASSET-B3_7C。科目生物，原第3章“细胞内物质和能量的转换”，真实小节3.7“环境因素对光合作用的影响”。教学目的：避免从CO2最低点直接推最佳储藏条件。必须画出的对象与正确关系：三曲线叠加须使用同一单位并声明底物/发酵模式假设；删除总CO2最低必等于有机物消耗最少/最佳保鲜的直接结论，不同底物RQ与组织伤害需另证据；曲线仅模型，不给未经测量的推荐氧浓度。图中文字逐项准确保留：O₂浓度；CO₂释放速率；有氧/发酵贡献；模型条件；不直接推保鲜最优。采用1200×800画布；双面板在手机可拆为纵向，渲染文字至少12 CSS px、目标14；线型/形状与颜色共同区分。白底高对比，科学层、箭头、文字分层，公式变量和单位不能装饰化。最终科学图优先用SVG/数学绘图库确定性制作。AI仅可作构图草稿，所有数值、几何、膜层和文字须重建核验后才能用于教学。标注版与练习留空版分开，只采用已有题给条件，不泄露检测答案。来源/理论核对：https://openstax.org/books/biology-2e/pages/8-2-the-light-dependent-reactions-of-photosynthesis；https://openstax.org/books/biology-2e/pages/8-3-using-light-energy-to-make-organic-molecules；https://openstax.org/books/biology-2e/pages/4-3-eukaryotic-cells。来源不等于2027考试范围确认；制作前重新核对。

**Complete English prompt**

Create an original Chinese-language teaching diagram titled "图 3-7c　O₂ 浓度对呼吸作用的影响（示意）". Unique ID ILL-ASSET-B3_7C; Biology, existing chapter 3, actual subsection 3.7 ("环境因素对光合作用的影响"). Scientific objects, relationships and exact constraints: Use consistent units before summing respiratory CO2 contributions and state substrate/fermentation assumptions. Remove the claim that minimum total CO2 necessarily means least organic-matter loss or optimal storage. Respiratory quotient, substrate and tissue damage need separate evidence; provide no invented recommended oxygen concentration. Required on-canvas Chinese labels, preserved verbatim: O₂浓度; CO₂释放速率; 有氧/发酵贡献; 模型条件; 不直接推保鲜最优. Use a 1200 by 800 canvas. Reflow panels vertically on mobile, with rendered labels at least12 CSS px, target14. Use shape/line style as well as color, white background and high contrast. Keep geometry, arrows and editable text on separate layers. The final scientific output must be constructed with SVG or deterministic mathematical plotting; an AI image is only a layout draft and cannot certify geometry, counts, membranes or formulas. Make annotated teaching and blank assessment variants separately, using only existing stated problem data. Recheck these theory/source references before production: https://openstax.org/books/biology-2e/pages/8-2-the-light-dependent-reactions-of-photosynthesis; https://openstax.org/books/biology-2e/pages/8-3-using-light-energy-to-make-organic-molecules; https://openstax.org/books/biology-2e/pages/4-3-eukaryotic-cells. These sources do not certify2027 exam scope or official marking rules.

**Negative Prompt**

不得反转箭头运算、偷换单位、改变题给数据、使用错误比例、吞掉绝对值、隐藏条件或伪造实测刻度。不使用乱码、伪官方标志、水印遮挡、未说明的缩写或“同上”。

No reversed operations/arrows, changed problem values, inconsistent units/scales, missing absolute-value bars, hidden conditions or invented measured axes. No garbled Chinese, fake official logos, obscuring watermarks, unexplained abbreviations or references to another prompt.
